### FILE: src/main/java/com/crypto/service/HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.java
package com.crypto.service;

import com.crypto.domain.SignalDecision;
import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;

/**
 * Pure FIX-154 BUY-context assessment. It deliberately returns effective
 * decisions separately and never mutates the JPA-managed context signals.
 */
@Service
public class HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService {
    public static final int HARD_CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD = 70;

    public Assessment assess(TradeSignal fiveMinute, boolean fiveFresh,
                             TradeSignal oneHour, boolean oneFresh) {
        if (!fiveFresh) return Assessment.reject("MISSING_5M_CONFIRMATION", Band.NOT_APPLICABLE, fiveMinute, oneHour);
        if (!oneFresh) return Assessment.reject("MISSING_1H_CONTEXT", Band.NOT_APPLICABLE, fiveMinute, oneHour);

        SignalDecision five = decision(fiveMinute);
        SignalDecision one = decision(oneHour);
        if (bearish(five)) {
            boolean hard = hard(oneHour);
            return Assessment.reject("5M_BEARISH_VETO", hard ? Band.HARD_BEARISH_CANDIDATE : band(oneHour), fiveMinute, oneHour)
                    .withHardConfirmation(hard);
        }
        if (bearish(one) && !qualityValid(oneHour)) {
            return Assessment.reject("1H_BEARISH_VETO", Band.NOT_TRUSTED, fiveMinute, oneHour);
        }
        if (hard(oneHour)) {
            return Assessment.reject("HTF_TRUSTED_BEARISH_VETO", Band.HARD_BEARISH_CANDIDATE, fiveMinute, oneHour);
        }
        if (soft(oneHour)) {
            return Assessment.allow(Band.SOFT_CONFLICT, five, SignalDecision.NEUTRAL, 25, fiveMinute, oneHour);
        }
        return Assessment.allow(qualityValid(oneHour) ? Band.NO_CONFLICT : Band.NOT_TRUSTED,
                five, one, null, fiveMinute, oneHour);
    }

    private Band band(TradeSignal one) {
        if (!qualityValid(one)) return Band.NOT_TRUSTED;
        if (hard(one)) return Band.HARD_BEARISH_CANDIDATE;
        if (soft(one)) return Band.SOFT_CONFLICT;
        return Band.NO_CONFLICT;
    }
    private boolean hard(TradeSignal s) { return qualityValid(s) && decision(s) == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL && confidence(s) >= HARD_CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD; }
    private boolean soft(TradeSignal s) { return qualityValid(s) && (decision(s) == SignalDecision.SELL || (decision(s) == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL && confidence(s) < HARD_CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD)); }
    private boolean qualityValid(TradeSignal s) { return s != null && Boolean.TRUE.equals(s.getDataQualityValid()); }
    private int confidence(TradeSignal s) { return s == null ? 0 : s.getConfidenceScore(); }
    private SignalDecision decision(TradeSignal s) { return s == null || s.getDecision() == null ? SignalDecision.NEUTRAL : s.getDecision(); }
    private boolean bearish(SignalDecision d) { return d == SignalDecision.SELL || d == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL; }

    public enum Band { NOT_APPLICABLE, NOT_TRUSTED, SOFT_CONFLICT, HARD_BEARISH_CANDIDATE, NO_CONFLICT }
    public record Assessment(boolean allowed, String code, Band band, SignalDecision effectiveFiveMinute,
                             SignalDecision effectiveOneHour, Integer maxInitialPercent,
                             boolean hardConfirmationPresent, TradeSignal rawFiveMinute, TradeSignal rawOneHour) {
        static Assessment reject(String code, Band band, TradeSignal five, TradeSignal one) {
            return new Assessment(false, code, band, decisionOf(five), decisionOf(one), null, false, five, one);
        }
        static Assessment allow(Band band, SignalDecision five, SignalDecision one, Integer cap, TradeSignal rawFive, TradeSignal rawOne) {
            return new Assessment(true, "HTF_CONTEXT_ALLOWED", band, five, one, cap, false, rawFive, rawOne);
        }
        Assessment withHardConfirmation(boolean value) { return new Assessment(allowed, code, band, effectiveFiveMinute, effectiveOneHour, maxInitialPercent, value, rawFiveMinute, rawOneHour); }
        private static SignalDecision decisionOf(TradeSignal s) { return s == null || s.getDecision() == null ? SignalDecision.NEUTRAL : s.getDecision(); }
    }
}

1197:         // FIX-091 / Fix 5: EntryAuthority is only a maximum size. The current signal has already
1198:         // travelled through the normal direct-candidate + validateBuy flow before this guard runs.
1199:         // The EntryQuality snapshot is created from the current TradeSignal, so the explicit cap is
1200:         // carried through the signal fields without inventing a parallel execution route.
1201:         if (q.entryAuthorityMaxPositionPercent() != null && q.entryAuthorityMaxPositionPercent() > 0) {
1202:             cap = Math.min(cap, q.entryAuthorityMaxPositionPercent());
1203:             if (q.transitionProbe()) {
1204:                 capReason.append("; BREAKOUT_TRANSITION probe authority caps exposure at ")
1205:                         .append(q.entryAuthorityMaxPositionPercent()).append("%");
1206:             }
1207:         }
1208:         if (q.recentRejectedHigh() != null) {
1209:             capReason.append("; recent rejection zone near ").append(q.recentRejectedHigh())
1210:                     .append(" reduced entry quality without creating a hard veto");
1211:         }
1212: 
1213:         int reduced = Math.min(decision.positionPercent(), cap);
1214:         if (reduced == decision.positionPercent()) return decision;
1215: 
1216:         return ExecutionDecision.allow(
1217:                 decision.source(),
1218:                 decision.code(),
1219:                 reduced,

1221:                         + "% so confirmation can add later instead of committing full size at once.",

1225:     /**
1226:      * FIX-119 ƒ?" Accumulated-evidence late-entry timing deferral.
1227:      *
1228:      * ACCUMULATED_EVIDENCE is opportunity memory. It may legitimately open a
1229:      * position without a current BUY when persistent WATCH evidence has matured;
1230:      * ICPUSDT #1702 is a proven example and must remain executable.
1231:      *
1232:      * The defect appears when that same WATCH-only authority reaches a price that
1233:      * Entry Quality already classifies as LATE_ENTRY. In the current implementation,
1234:      * scores 50-54 remain executable with reduced sizing, including cases where a
1235:      * recent rejection-zone penalty was deliberately floored at the 50-point chase
1236:      * cutoff. Historical BTCUSDT #1701, EDUUSDT #1716 and UNIUSDT #1731 demonstrate
1237:      * that this can convert a valid opportunity into a poorly timed initial entry.
1238:      *
1239:      * FIX-119 therefore defers only when all of the following are simultaneously true:
1240:      *   - the candidate is an allowed ACCUMULATED_EVIDENCE initial entry;
1241:      *   - Entry Quality is LATE_ENTRY;
1242:      *   - accumulated fresh 1m evidence contains zero BUY observations; and
1243:      *   - fresh 5m setup context is not BUY/STRONG_BUY.
1244:      *
1245:      * The result stays BUILDING rather than being rejected. Existing opportunity
1246:      * evidence is preserved by saveOpportunity(), so subsequent signals can release
1247:      * the opportunity when price geometry improves or fresh BUY authority appears.
1248:      *
1249:      * Good controls intentionally preserved:
1250:      *   - ICPUSDT #1702: 0 BUY / 10 WATCH but ACCEPTABLE_ENTRY 55 -> unchanged.
1251:      *   - SUIUSDT #1696: LATE_ENTRY 50 but accumulated BUY evidence and 5m BUY
1252:      *     -> unchanged.
1253:      *
1254:      * A 1h BUY by itself does not bypass this guard. EDUUSDT #1716 had 1h BUY /
1255:      * BALANCED_STRONG but remained a badly timed WATCH-only accumulated entry.
1256:      */
1257:     private ExecutionDecision applyAccumulatedLateEntryTimingGuard(
1258:             TradeSignal signal,
1259:             ExecutionDecision decision,
1260:             Evidence evidence,
1261:             EntryQuality entryQuality) {
1262: 
1263:         if (decision == null
1264:                 || !decision.allowed()
1265:                 || !"ACCUMULATED_EVIDENCE".equals(decision.source())
1266:                 || evidence == null
1267:                 || entryQuality == null) {
1268:             return decision;
1269:         }
1270: 
1271:         if (!"LATE_ENTRY".equals(entryQuality.classification())) {
1272:             return decision;
1273:         }
1274: 
1275:         boolean hasAccumulatedBuyEvidence = evidence.buyCount() > 0;
1276:         boolean hasFreshFiveMinuteBuy = isBullish(evidence.fiveMinute());
1277: 
1278:         if (hasAccumulatedBuyEvidence || hasFreshFiveMinuteBuy) {
1279:             return decision;
1280:         }
1281: 
1282:         String rejectionContext = entryQuality.recentRejectedHigh() == null
1283:                 ? "none"
1284:                 : entryQuality.recentRejectedHigh()
1285:                         + " (distance="
1286:                         + format(entryQuality.distanceFromRejectedHighPercent())
1287:                         + "%)";

1297:                 signal == null ? null : signal.getSymbol(),
1298:                 signal == null ? null : signal.getGeneratedAt(),
1299:                 signal == null ? null : signal.getLatestPrice(),
1300:                 entryQuality.score(),
1301:                 entryQuality.classification(),
1302:                 rejectionContext,
1303:                 evidence.buyCount(),
1304:                 evidence.watchCount(),
1305:                 evidence.neutralCount(),
1306:                 evidence.evidenceScore(),
1307:                 evidence.averageScore(),
1308:                 evidence.averageConfidence(),
1309:                 evidence.fiveMinute(),
1310:                 evidence.oneHour());
1311: 
1312:         return ExecutionDecision.building(
1313:                 "ACCUMULATED_LATE_ENTRY_DEFERRED",
1314:                 "Accumulated evidence remains valid, but execution is deferred because "
1315:                         + "Entry Quality is " + entryQuality.score() + "/100 ("
1316:                         + entryQuality.classification() + "), accumulated fresh evidence "
1317:                         + "contains no BUY observation, and fresh 5m setup context is "

1320:                                 ? ""

1322:                                         + entryQuality.recentRejectedHigh()
1323:                                         + " is only "
1324:                                         + format(entryQuality.distanceFromRejectedHighPercent())
1325:                                         + "% from the current entry price.")

1336:     private ExecutionDecision exceptionalStrengthProbeDecision(
1337:             TradeSignal current, Evidence e, HardRiskBlock block) {
1338:         if (current == null || block == null || !block.blocked()) return null;
1339:         if (!"BTC_CONTEXT_BLOCKED".equals(block.code())) return null;
1340: 
1341:         // BTC is allowed to reduce exposure here, not to manufacture extra score.
1342:         // Every other risk authority remains mandatory.
1343:         if (!current.isStrategyEntryAllowed()
1344:                 || !current.isDerivativesEntryAllowed()
1345:                 || !current.isLiquidityEntryAllowed()
1346:                 || !current.isAtrImmediateEntryAllowed()) return null;
1347:         if (current.getLatestPrice() == null || current.getStopLoss() == null || current.getTakeProfit() == null
1348:                 || current.getStopLoss().signum() <= 0 || current.getTakeProfit().signum() <= 0) return null;
1349: 
1350:         if (current.getOriginalDecision() != SignalDecision.STRONG_BUY) return null;
1351:         if (current.getDecision() != SignalDecision.WATCH && !isBullish(current.getDecision())) return null;
1352:         if (current.getTotalScore() < EXCEPTIONAL_PROBE_MIN_SCORE
1353:                 || current.getTrendScore() < EXCEPTIONAL_PROBE_MIN_TREND
1354:                 || current.getMomentumScore() < EXCEPTIONAL_PROBE_MIN_MOMENTUM
1355:                 || current.getVolumeScore() < EXCEPTIONAL_PROBE_MIN_VOLUME) return null;
1356: 
1357:         // This route is specifically a reversal probe, not a generic high-score bypass.
1358:         // Require a fresh bearish -> exceptional bullish acceleration on the same 1m stream.
1359:         TradeSignal prior = recentBearishReversalAnchor(current);
1360:         if (prior == null) return null;
1361:         SignalDecision priorEffective = strongerDecision(prior.getDecision(), prior.getOriginalDecision());
1362:         int scoreJump = current.getTotalScore() - prior.getTotalScore();
1363:         if (scoreJump < EXCEPTIONAL_PROBE_MIN_SCORE_JUMP) return null;
1364: 
1365:         TradeSignal five = latestAtOrBefore(current, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL, FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE);
1366:         TradeSignal oneHour = latestAtOrBefore(current, TREND_INTERVAL, ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE);
1367:         if (five == null || oneHour == null) return null;
1368:         if (isBearish(five.getDecision()) || isBearish(five.getOriginalDecision())
1369:                 || isBearish(oneHour.getDecision()) || isBearish(oneHour.getOriginalDecision())) return null;
1370: 
1371:         SignalDecision btcDecision = current.getBtcContextDecision();
1372:         int percent = btcDecision == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL
1373:                 ? EXCEPTIONAL_PROBE_BTC_STRONG_SELL_PERCENT
1374:                 : EXCEPTIONAL_PROBE_BTC_SELL_PERCENT;
1375: 
1376:         return ExecutionDecision.allow(
1377:                 "EXCEPTIONAL_STRENGTH_PROBE",
1378:                 "BTC_CONFLICT_REDUCED_PROBE",
1379:                 percent,
1380:                 "High-conviction reversal qualified for a reduced BTC-conflict probe: prior="
1381:                         + priorEffective + " " + prior.getTotalScore() + " -> current="
1382:                         + current.getOriginalDecision() + " " + current.getTotalScore()
1383:                         + " (score jump +" + scoreJump + "), trend=" + current.getTrendScore()
1384:                         + ", momentum=" + current.getMomentumScore() + ", volume=" + current.getVolumeScore()
1385:                         + ", 1m=" + current.getDecision() + ", 5m=" + five.getDecision()
1386:                         + ", 1h=" + oneHour.getDecision() + ", BTC=" + (btcDecision == null ? "BEARISH" : btcDecision)
1387:                         + ". BTC weakness reduces exposure to " + percent
1388:                         + "% instead of increasing the technical score or bypassing asset-timeframe confirmation.",
1389:                 e
1390:         );

2221:     private HardRiskBlock nonAtrHardRiskBlock(TradeSignal signal) {
2222:         if (!signal.isStrategyEntryAllowed()) {

2224:         }
2225:         if (!signal.isBtcContextEntryAllowed()) {

2227:         }
2228:         if (!signal.isDerivativesEntryAllowed()) {
2229:             return HardRiskBlock.block("DERIVATIVES_BLOCKED", "Funding/open-interest positioning issued an entry veto.");
2230:         }

2232:         // when the wall is genuinely strong/relevant. Weakening walls no longer reach this hard veto.
2233:         if (!signal.isLiquidityEntryAllowed()
2234:                 && signal.getLiquidityStatus() == LiquidityContextStatus.TARGET_BLOCKED) {
2235:             return HardRiskBlock.block("STRONG_TARGET_WALL",
2236:                     "A strong nearby target-side wall is still active; execution remains blocked until fresh order-book evidence changes.");
2237:         }

2241:     private Evidence evidence(TradeSignal current) {
2242:         // FIX-122: initial-entry scope only; additions keep their original evidence.
2243:         if (stopLossEvidence != null) stopLossEvidence.requireCurrent(current);
2244:         Instant cutoff = current.getGeneratedAt().minus(EVIDENCE_WINDOW);
2245:         List<TradeSignal> recent = recentSignals(current.getSymbol(), EXECUTION_INTERVAL, current.getGeneratedAt());
2246: 
2247:         int buy = 0, watch = 0, neutral = 0, bearish = 0, evidenceScore = 0;
2248:         int scoreTotal = 0, confidenceTotal = 0, qualityCount = 0;
2249:         int health = OPPORTUNITY_HEALTH_START;
2250:         Instant lastBearishAt = null;
2251:         Instant oldestObservedAt = null;
2252:         List<Long> signalIds = new ArrayList<>();
2253:         List<TradeSignal> observedSequence = new ArrayList<>();
2254: 
2255:         for (TradeSignal signal : recent) {
2256:             if (signal.getGeneratedAt() == null || signal.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(current.getGeneratedAt())) continue;
2257:             if (signal.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(cutoff)) break;
2258:             if (stopLossEvidence != null && !stopLossEvidence.eligible(signal, "EVIDENCE")) continue;
2259: 
2260:             if (oldestObservedAt == null || signal.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(oldestObservedAt)) {
2261:                 oldestObservedAt = signal.getGeneratedAt();
2262:             }
2263: 
2264:             observedSequence.add(signal);
2265: 
2266:             SignalDecision decision = signal.getDecision();
2267:             SignalDecision original = signal.getOriginalDecision();
2268:             SignalDecision effective = strongerDecision(decision, original);
2269: 
2270:             if (effective == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL) {
2271:                 bearish++;
2272:                 health += scaledHealthContribution(HEALTH_1M_STRONG_SELL, signal);
2273:                 if (lastBearishAt == null) lastBearishAt = signal.getGeneratedAt();
2274:                 break; // a strong bearish 1m event is still a true memory boundary
2275:             }
2276: 
2277:             if (effective == SignalDecision.SELL) {
2278:                 bearish++;
2279:                 evidenceScore -= WEAK_SELL_EVIDENCE_PENALTY;
2280:                 health += scaledHealthContribution(HEALTH_1M_SELL, signal);
2281:                 if (lastBearishAt == null) lastBearishAt = signal.getGeneratedAt();
2282:                 continue;
2283:             }
2284: 
2285:             if (isBullish(effective)) {
2286:                 buy++;
2287:                 evidenceScore += 3;
2288:                 health += scaledHealthContribution(HEALTH_1M_BUY, signal);
2289:                 if (signal.getId() != null) {
2290:                     signalIds.add(signal.getId());
2291:                 }
2292:             } else if (decision == SignalDecision.WATCH
2293:                     && signal.getTotalScore() >= WATCH_EVIDENCE_MIN_SCORE
2294:                     && signal.getConfidenceScore() >= WATCH_EVIDENCE_MIN_CONFIDENCE) {
2295:                 watch++;
2296:                 evidenceScore += 1;
2297:                 health += scaledHealthContribution(HEALTH_1M_WATCH, signal);
2298:                 if (signal.getId() != null) {
2299:                     signalIds.add(signal.getId());
2300:                 }
2301:             } else if (decision == SignalDecision.NEUTRAL) {
2302:                 neutral++;
2303:                 health += HEALTH_1M_NEUTRAL;
2304:             }
2305: 
2306:             if (decision == SignalDecision.WATCH || isBullish(decision)) {
2307:                 scoreTotal += signal.getTotalScore();
2308:                 confidenceTotal += signal.getConfidenceScore();
2309:                 qualityCount++;
2310:             }
2311:         }
2312: 
2313:         int evidenceMomentum = calculateEvidenceMomentum(observedSequence);
2314:         health += evidenceMomentum;
2315: 
2316:         TradeSignal five = latestAtOrBefore(current, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL, FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE);
2317:         TradeSignal one = latestAtOrBefore(current, TREND_INTERVAL, ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE);
2318: 
2319:         // Higher timeframe evidence actively restores or weakens opportunity health.
2320:         // This is deliberately generic: no symbol-specific values are used.
2321:         health += timeframeHealthContribution(five, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL);
2322:         health += timeframeHealthContribution(one, TREND_INTERVAL);
2323: 
2324:         long ageMinutes = oldestObservedAt == null ? 0
2325:                 : Math.max(0, Duration.between(oldestObservedAt, current.getGeneratedAt()).toMinutes());
2326:         // Slow decay: fresh bullish evidence should be able to recover faster than age destroys it.
2327:         health -= Math.min(6, (int) (ageMinutes / 10));
2328:         evidenceScore = Math.max(0, evidenceScore);
2329:         health = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, health));
2330: 
2331:         int previousHealth = currentOpportunity(current.getSymbol(), List.of("BUILDING", "WEAKENING", "BLOCKED", "CONFIRMED"))
2332:                 // FIX-122: do not compare fresh health to a pre-stop opportunity snapshot.
2333:                 .filter(o -> stopLossEvidence == null || stopLossEvidence.eligible(o.getLatestSignal(), "PREVIOUS_HEALTH"))
2334:                 .map(ExecutionOpportunity::getOpportunityHealth)
2335:                 .orElse(OPPORTUNITY_HEALTH_START);
2336:         int healthMomentum = health - previousHealth;
2337: 
2338:         return new Evidence(
2339:                 buy + watch + neutral + bearish,
2340:                 buy,
2341:                 watch,
2342:                 neutral,
2343:                 bearish,
2344:                 evidenceScore,
2345:                 health,
2346:                 healthMomentum,
2347:                 evidenceMomentum,
2348:                 qualityCount == 0 ? 0 : Math.round((float) scoreTotal / qualityCount),
2349:                 qualityCount == 0 ? 0 : Math.round((float) confidenceTotal / qualityCount),
2350:                 five == null ? null : five.getDecision(),
2351:                 one == null ? null : one.getDecision(),
2352:                 lastBearishAt,
2353:                 signalIds.stream().filter(Objects::nonNull).toList()
2354:         );
2355:     }

2357:     private int calculateEvidenceMomentum(List<TradeSignal> observedDescending) {
2358:         if (observedDescending == null || observedDescending.size() < 2) return 0;
2359: 
2360:         List<TradeSignal> sequence = new ArrayList<>(observedDescending);
2361:         Collections.reverse(sequence); // oldest -> newest
2362:         if (sequence.size() > EVIDENCE_MOMENTUM_WINDOW) {
2363:             sequence = new ArrayList<>(sequence.subList(sequence.size() - EVIDENCE_MOMENTUM_WINDOW, sequence.size()));
2364:         }
2365: 
2366:         int momentum = 0;
2367:         for (int i = 1; i < sequence.size(); i++) {
2368:             TradeSignal previous = sequence.get(i - 1);
2369:             TradeSignal current = sequence.get(i);
2370:             int previousStrength = decisionStrength(strongerDecision(previous.getDecision(), previous.getOriginalDecision()));
2371:             int currentStrength = decisionStrength(strongerDecision(current.getDecision(), current.getOriginalDecision()));
2372:             int delta = currentStrength - previousStrength;
2373:             int recencyWeight = Math.min(3, i);
2374:             momentum += delta * 3 * recencyWeight;
2375:         }
2376: 
2377:         TradeSignal latest = sequence.get(sequence.size() - 1);
2378:         int latestStrength = decisionStrength(strongerDecision(latest.getDecision(), latest.getOriginalDecision()));
2379:         momentum += latestStrength * 2;
2380:         return Math.max(-EVIDENCE_MOMENTUM_CAP, Math.min(EVIDENCE_MOMENTUM_CAP, momentum));
2381:     }

2383:     private int decisionStrength(SignalDecision decision) {
2384:         if (decision == null) return 0;
2385:         return switch (decision) {
2386:             case STRONG_BUY -> 2;
2387:             case BUY, WATCH -> 1;
2388:             case NEUTRAL -> 0;
2389:             case SELL -> -1;
2390:             case STRONG_SELL -> -2;
2391:         };
2392:     }

2394:     private int timeframeHealthContribution(TradeSignal signal, String interval) {
2395:         if (signal == null) return 0;
2396:         SignalDecision decision = strongerDecision(signal.getDecision(), signal.getOriginalDecision());
2397:         int base;
2398:         if (CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL.equals(interval)) {
2399:             base = switch (decision) {
2400:                 case BUY, STRONG_BUY -> HEALTH_5M_BUY;
2401:                 case WATCH -> HEALTH_5M_WATCH;
2402:                 case SELL -> HEALTH_5M_SELL;
2403:                 case STRONG_SELL -> HEALTH_5M_STRONG_SELL;
2404:                 default -> 0;
2405:             };
2406:         } else {
2407:             base = switch (decision) {
2408:                 case BUY, STRONG_BUY -> HEALTH_1H_BUY;
2409:                 case WATCH -> HEALTH_1H_WATCH;
2410:                 case SELL -> HEALTH_1H_SELL;
2411:                 case STRONG_SELL -> HEALTH_1H_STRONG_SELL;
2412:                 default -> 0;
2413:             };
2414:         }
2415:         return scaledHealthContribution(base, signal);
2416:     }
2417: 
2418:     private int scaledHealthContribution(int base, TradeSignal signal) {
2419:         if (base == 0 || signal == null) return 0;
2420:         int quality = Math.round((signal.getTotalScore() + signal.getConfidenceScore()) / 2.0f);
2421:         double factor = quality >= 80 ? 1.25 : quality >= 70 ? 1.0 : quality >= 60 ? 0.8 : 0.6;
2422:         return (int) Math.round(base * factor);
2423:     }

2442:     private TradeSignal recentBearishReversalAnchor(TradeSignal current) {
2443:         if (current == null || current.getGeneratedAt() == null) return null;
2444:         Instant cutoff = current.getGeneratedAt().minus(EXCEPTIONAL_PROBE_REVERSAL_LOOKBACK);
2445:         return recentSignals(current.getSymbol(), EXECUTION_INTERVAL, current.getGeneratedAt()).stream()
2446:                 .filter(signal -> signal != null && signal.getGeneratedAt() != null)
2447:                 .filter(signal -> signal.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(current.getGeneratedAt())
2448:                         && !signal.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(cutoff))
2449:                 .filter(signal -> isBearish(strongerDecision(signal.getDecision(), signal.getOriginalDecision())))
2450:                 .filter(signal -> signal.getTotalScore() <= EXCEPTIONAL_PROBE_MAX_PRIOR_SCORE)
2451:                 .max(java.util.Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt))
2452:                 .orElse(null);

## FINDINGS

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The cohort remains exactly IDs **182, 172, 162, 158, 143, 137**. No substitution was made.
- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The permitted `db/CODEX` folder has no query results. Entry quality for every frozen trade is therefore **unverifiable**, not “poor,” “late,” or rule-compliant.
- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The current source re-scores entry quality at the fresh execution price immediately before wallet BUY. It blocks scores below 50, permits 50–69 only at a 25% cap, 70–84 at 40%, and 85+ at 50%, subject to stricter guards/caps. [ExecutionIntelligenceService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/ExecutionIntelligenceService.java:865)
- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** A BUY is also rejected if its fresh execution price is at/below stop or at/above target. [ExecutionIntelligenceService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/ExecutionIntelligenceService.java:1150)
- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The stored `paper_position.entry_price` is the fresh execution price, but it does not persist the calculated entry-quality score or its components. [PaperTradingService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/PaperTradingService.java:377)
- **AGENT INFERENCE:** Reconstructing entry quality needs the entry signal, contemporaneous 1m signals/opportunity state, entry price, ATR/risk-reward inputs, and the actual runtime version. Position data alone cannot establish the score.
- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** There is a status contradiction: `AGENTS.md` describes FIX-154 as not implemented, while current workspace source contains its assessment service. This establishes neither deployment nor applicability to these six trades; runtime/version evidence is required.

## EVIDENCE

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Entry-quality score components are: expansion from a bounded rolling/opportunity reference, ATR extension, opportunity age, execution-price risk/reward, recent-rejection proximity, high-volatility regime, stop exposure, and a qualifying breakout bonus. [ExecutionIntelligenceService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/ExecutionIntelligenceService.java:935)
- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Entry authority also requires structural, strategy, BTC, derivatives, liquidity, ATR, and higher-timeframe checks; entry quality is a downstream guard, not sole authority. [ExecutionIntelligenceService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/ExecutionIntelligenceService.java:2221)
- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The supplied snapshot UTC `2026-10-20 15:00:04` converts to **KSA `2026-10-20 18:00:04`**, while the charter states `18:00:02 KSA`. This two-second metadata discrepancy does not change the frozen selection.
- **EXTERNAL RESEARCH:** None.
- **AGENT INFERENCE:** No causal finding—late chase, stale signal, weak risk/reward, quality-gate bypass, or valid loss—can be made for any selected trade yet.
